| REG-339 | Cross | **Three paths the demo-data audit caught** (2026-09-04, the read-only pass over local.db that checked every state the UI shows against the records a real user action would have left behind). (1) **A task completed or re-dated from a popover left no stamp**: `completeTask` and `setTaskDue` (app/leads/actions.ts — the listing's Tasks popover and the Overview's My Tasks card) wrote `done` / `due_at` alone, while the Tasks tab's `updateTask` stamps `updated_at` on every edit; the Activity feed (REG-191) prints "You completed the task …" only when `updated_at` sits more than a minute past `created_at`, so a completion demoed from either popover never reached the feed — the same gap the audit found on every done task in the base state. Both now stamp `updated_at`, as `updateTask` does. (2) **A resume into Contacted hid every contact log on the Notes tab**: the stage-origin logs (REG-265) were read from the lead's LATEST `to_stage = Contacted` row whatever its payload, and "Resume at Contacted?" (REG-132) writes a `{quick:true}` row with no logs — the tab then showed none although the wizard's own row still held them. The loader now takes the latest Contacted row whose payload is a wizard payload (`isWizardPayload`, the test `mergeStageData` already applies), skipping quick moves. (3) **The Deleted Leads sheet printed the name as saved**: REG-329's read-time `titleCaseName` runs in `buildLeadRows`, which the sheet bypasses (it renders the `Lead` row itself), so a lead saved as "test" read "test" there and "Test" everywhere else; the name cell, its checkbox label and the Delete Forever subtitle now go through `titleCaseName`. Code only — the data repair the same audit prescribes is logged on its own. | bug fix |
| REG-340 | Move stage | **The From System lists read the lead's own quotes and contracts** (data audit 2026-09-04, spec C4: the window hard-coded "OM-10001 · Approved on 19 Aug" / "Signed on 19 Aug" for all 36 leads, so it disagreed with the Quotes and Contracts tabs and with the repaired data). The window now reads the lead's records when it opens (`fetchStageDocuments`, app/leads/quotes-actions.ts — one read, dates as epoch ms): **Quote Sent** lists quotes in Approved or Sent to Client as title · number · amount · "Approved on <date>"; **Won** lists Signed quotes ("Signed on" = the Signed move's updated stamp, a quote has no signed column) and Signed contracts named by their signed copy. The payment split is computed on the selected quote's own total (the lead's budget only until the rows land); the "Project created …" snackbar names the selected quote's number and drops the suffix for an uploaded quote. Payloads keep storing the document's NAME: the untouched defaults (SYSTEM_QUOTE / SYSTEM_CONTRACT) resolve to the first row the lead actually has, or to nothing — so a lead with no approved quote must choose or upload, as validateStage already demands. **Closes REG-325's open state**: with nothing to list the tab draws the frame's empty state (780:90472) — "No system generated quotes." and "Create Quote ›", which closes the window and opens the lead's Quotes tab with `?new=1` (the Overview keeps its Qanvast origin). Won's contract list has no drawn empty state; it uses the same frame with "contracts" / "Create Contract ›" → the Contracts tab `?new=1` (invented by analogy). Nothing is drawn while the rows are loading — never a flash of the empty state. The Upload flows are untouched. | bug fix + mock |
| REG-341 | Base state | **Open tasks' due dates ride the calendar across a reset** (2026-09-04, from the demo-data audit). `scripts/seed.ts` dated its tasks relative to the day it ran (27 Aug) and `pnpm db:baseline` froze them, so eight days on every dated open task in the base state was overdue — "Due today" was unreachable and no dated task ever reached "Upcoming" on the My Tasks card (858:24989) — and Reset prototype to base (REG-142) put the same stale dates straight back. The snapshot now writes `demoDay` beside `takenAt`: the calendar day the base state was curated on, in Asia/Kolkata (the demo machine's clock, and the midnight the data repair rebases due dates to). `restoreBaseline()` — behind Maison AI › Reset prototype to base and `scripts/restore-baseline.ts` — then moves `lead_tasks.due_at` on every OPEN task by the whole days between that day and the day of the reset, inside the same transaction as the inserts: a task due on the demo day is due today, one due in two days is still upcoming, and the two deliberately overdue examples are exactly as overdue as they were curated. Whole days, not seconds, so the hour a real pick writes (10:00) reads the same. Done tasks keep the day they were actually due; every other stamp (created, seen, updated, logs, quotes, files) stays absolute; a fixture without `demoDay` restores unshifted. The base state still ages between resets — a reset is what re-anchors it. | bug fix |
| REG-342 | Lead Details | **The info column's expanded blocks show their glyphs** (user 2026-09-04: "there are icons when this panel is collapsed but not there in expanded view"). Collaborators, Lead info, Property info and Other info carry the same 20px Light glyph their REG-304 strip tile uses (people_alt · info · home · sticky_note_2), before the title in the block head. The Contact block has no heading, so nothing to carry. The mock's expanded column (858:24783) draws no glyphs — user-directed. | user-directed |
| REG-343 | Cross | **The demo data now says what the screens show** (user 2026-09-04: "I checked a lead named Farah Ahmad — the stage is Contacted, two members are assigned, but in the activity there is nothing … when we develop it should be correctly built without any mock data as is … this will be a demo prototype to show it to studios"). The prototype derives every card, duration and Activity line from the lead's own records (REG-191), but the seeds wrote leads straight into a stage, so 14 live leads past New had NO stage log at all: the Lead stages card showed "--" for every span, the Notes tab had no contact log to surface, and Activity showed only "Lead created". A read-only audit of all 36 leads across seven dimensions (stage trail, members, tasks, quotes/contracts, files/notes, questionnaire/returns/imports, listing fields) found 56 inconsistencies; 51 survived an adversarial verify. `scripts/repair-demo-data.ts` (`pnpm db:repair`) writes the records a real user action would have left, idempotently — deterministic ids from a hash of lead + kind, every timestamp derived from the lead's own creation: the stage chain each lead's journey implies (a day to first contact, three to the meeting, four to the quote, five to the close, compressed when the lead moved faster) carrying the full WizardData payload the wizard writes; `assigned_at` on all 32 assignments; a quote for every Quote Sent lead and a signed quote + contract for every Won lead, approved before the hop that needed them; the Lost move, task and outreach date behind every Qanvast return (a rejected return hands the lead back to the stage it was parked at); an owner for a lead past New that had none; contact logs for the quick moves and for payloads whose picker defaults predated the lead; records shifted after the lead they belong to; completion stamps on done tasks; open due dates rebased around the run day; the skipped rows an import claims. Test junk is purged (4 trashed "test" leads, 10 keyboard-mash tasks, 2 empty draft quotes). Fourteen verification queries run after the write and the script exits non-zero if any finds a row. Run it after any seeding, then `pnpm db:baseline`. | bug fix |
| REG-344 | Cross | **Mobile is the mock again, to the pixel** (user 2026-09-04: "I wanted that standard pixel perfect responsive build" … "the header and bottom nav … cards, page headers, filters, fab are correct in the figma mock" — 1082:23305). Two rules had been quietly reshaping every mobile screen. (1) **The touch floor grew the drawn control, not the target.** REG-273 raised small controls to a 44px BOX (`min-block-size`/`min-inline-size`, or width/height plus a negative margin); the border and fill sit on that box, so the mock's 36px header buttons, the filter and the "All Leads" pill all rendered 44 — 4px left of where the mock puts them and 8px taller — and every card row inflated with them. The drawn control now keeps the mock's size and an invisible `::after` overlay carries the 44 target (`max(100%, 44px)`, centred), in `app/globals.css` for the DS families and `components/om/icon-button.module.css` for the om button, so touch changes nothing about layout. Verified: a press 3px outside a 36px button still reaches it. (2) **REG-314 is reversed** — ≤767 read body/m at 16/24 over the mock's 14, from the responsive brief's "minimum readable body text"; the owner has since made the mock the target, and 16 made every text row 4px taller so no card could overlay it. The token keeps one value at every width; iOS input zoom is still handled on the fields themselves (REG-306). (3) The lead card's name row and info line are now ONE block 4px apart — the mock's "CellDataGroup" (1082:25586) — instead of two children of the card's 12px rhythm. **Result at 375**: header buttons 36 at x227/275/323, filter 36 at x323, pill 36 at y136, card 173 tall with its group at 48, rule at 108 and foot at 121 — every value the mock's. Desktop is untouched (the touch rules are `hover: none`, the type change was mobile-only): a 12-page sweep at 1440 and 375 shows no horizontal overflow and no clipped text on either. \| user-directed \|
| REG-345 | Lead Details | **Only Overview carries the lead's identity at ≤767** (user 2026-09-04, ideation 1144:2020 frames 2a / 2b). The L2 header spent four rows before any content: breadcrumb and name, the chips (which ran off-screen), the stage pill with Convert to Project, then the tabs — on a 812-tall phone that left barely a screen for the tab itself. A `compact` header (every tab but Overview) drops the chips and the stage / Convert pair and keeps its cluster — the kebab alone — on the title row: 108px of chrome instead of 156, so every tab gains 48px of content. Overview keeps all of it, and desktop is unchanged at every width. \| user-directed \|
| REG-346 | Cross | **Three desktop tables become lists at ≤767** (user 2026-09-04, ideation frames 2b / 3c / 3j). Tasks, Lead Import History and Qanvast Ops › Leads each held their desktop column floor (1394, 1316 and a fixed grid) inside a 375 card, so every row scrolled sideways and read as a fragment. Each becomes the ideation's list, by stylesheet only — no second markup. **Tasks**: the status ring and the title lead, the due date and the member sit under them, the row kebab is pinned right and permanent (it was hover-only, which a finger can never raise), and Creator, Status, Tags and the select column stand down to the kebab's menu. **Imports**: the file name leads, then the date, "of N — M imported", the error note and the skipped-records link; the six column widths moved from an inline `flex` (which CSS cannot override) to a `--col` custom property. **Qanvast Ops**: the grid collapses to one column per lead and its row actions stop depending on hover. Desktop keeps every table exactly as mocked. \| user-directed \|
| REG-347 | Cross | **Every modal, sheet, drawer and popover audited on a phone** (user 2026-09-05, after the ideated mobile set 1144:2020). Six agents opened each surface at 375 and measured it; 38 findings, verified before acting. **Unreachable controls (blockers):** an anchored menu clamped with a constant, so one opened low on the page ran past the viewport and its last items were drawn under the fixed dock — the `Dropdown` now reports its own top and the cap subtracts that and the dock; the Tasks toolbar wraps at ≤767 so its kebab fell to the LEFT and threw its right-aligned 200px menu off the screen — it is pushed flush right; the tasks bulk bar was told to wrap while keeping `height: 50px`, so "Clear selection" sat below the viewport; the review sheet's merge table froze a label column left and "Merged result" right, leaving a 113px window on a 130px column, so the New column could not be reached at any scroll offset — every comparison table now stacks, as the twin table already did; the file viewer's stage never subtracted the dock, so the zoom bar was drawn behind it; and the Notes tab's 24px clearance is a desktop rule (one card that never scrolls), which on a phone left the contact-log editor's Cancel / Save under the dock. **Also fixed:** the Qanvast board kept its six desktop tracks and pinned every cell to one of them, so collapsing the row alone changed nothing and each row drew over the next (the card, the pins and the fixed 64px height all give way, the row actions join the stack instead of floating over it, and the two numeric cells name themselves); the stage menu's assumed footprint was stale by 44px, hiding "Lost" under the dock; the Assign popover sat under the FAB, which covered a member's checkbox; the Choose Template footer needed 404px in a 343px sheet and overflowed to the left; the import wizard's upload toast covered "Re-upload File"; the Notes New ▾ started a draft without pushing the pane, so the tab's primary action looked inert; the pane toolbar's mobile rules named `.toolbar`, a class the markup dropped at REG-292, so the Maison AI chip was clipped away; Manage Tags' hard 600px height stranded it at half a screen; and the shared date picker — the one surface REG-344 never reached — had a 24px pager and 32px day cells with no touch overlay. Imports' own card order, a regression from REG-346, is corrected. Desktop is untouched at every width. | bug fix |
| REG-348 | Cross | **The two gaps REG-347 left open, closed** (user 2026-09-05: "fix all of them"). **Multi-select had no entry point on a phone**: REG-346 hid the Tasks select column, and with it the bulk bar's Duplicate, Save as Template and Delete — the whole multi-row path was unreachable at ≤767. The checkbox is back, pinned to the row's left with the title clearing it, so a long title can never push it onto a line of its own; selecting raises the bulk bar, which grows to fit its wrapped actions (REG-347) and clears the dock. **The file viewer's side panels ignored Escape**: they are full-screen sheets at ≤767 and every other sheet in the app closes on it, but Comments and Information closed only from their own X. Both now route through the shared Escape layer (`components/om/surface.ts`), which also keeps them behind whatever opens on top of them. | user-directed |

Only the name text navigated, so the phone line and the space beside it were dead — the same gap the stage cell had (REG-332). The cell now opens the lead from anywhere in it, while its own controls keep their actions: the selection checkbox and Copy phone number bubble their click and the guard lets them through. The name underlines on hover only (user, same day: "on resting state we don't need the underline, only on hover") — a resting row stays quiet, and the underline joins the primary colour as the cue. The table is a ≥768 surface (≤767 is LeadCards, REG-299), so a hover cue is the right one; on a touch tablet the whole cell being tappable carries it instead. | user-directed |
| REG-350 | Qanvast Ops | **A scrollbar that scrolled nothing** (user 2026-09-05: "I see this vertical scroll bar that does not do anything"). The vertical axis is now explicitly hidden — nothing needs to leave the card that way: the row's hover actions sit inside their row, and the Customise your view popover is `position: fixed`. The listing's own scroller has the same one-axis declaration but no rounding gap today; it is left alone because its row kebab menus are in-flow and do rely on that scroll. | bug fix |

It now carries the listing's cell exactly (REG-332 / REG-349) — the whole Name cell opens the lead, the name reads as a link on the ROW's hover (primary + underline, nothing at rest), and the phone gains the copy button that appears with the row and answers "Copied to clipboard" through the same `phoneDisplay` format. The cell's own controls keep their click, so Copy and the hover buttons still do their own thing. \| user-directed \|

The strip kept the DESKTOP offset (8 + 72) on a phone — a 20px gap under the old top bar that nobody caught, and once the bar went (REG-362) an 80px band of white above a strip stuck 80px down the screen while the header scrolled away behind it. The mobile rule is doubled too now. | bug fix |
| REG-368 | Cross | **Two sticky tab-strip bugs** (user 2026-09-06). **The Lead Details strip was being painted over:** the L2 header keeps `z-index: 20` from its sticky rule, and at ≤767 it is `position: relative` but kept the layer — so a header that no longer sticks still drew OVER the strip stuck beneath it, sliding in behind and cutting the tabs' first pixels off as the page scrolled. It claims no layer there now. **The Qanvast Ops strip did not stick at all:** it had none of Lead Details' treatment, and once given it, it still scrolled away — the `<div>` that exists only to intercept the inert tabs' clicks was a real box exactly the strip's height, so the sticky element had nowhere to travel inside it. The wrapper is `display: contents`; the strip sticks under this page's own header (8 + 68 on the desktop, the bar + 60 on a phone) and its rule is doubled, like Lead Details', so om/tabs' `position: relative` cannot win it back. | bug fix |

Scrolled (1186:2813 "Onscroll") the header is gone and the strip alone is pinned at the top, still carrying that one shadow. The build drew TWO — the ≤767 rule gave every page header `level-1` on its plate (REG-298) while the shared strip carries `level-1` of its own — so on the two pages that have both, a shadow sat between the header and the tabs and another under the tabs. The shadow now belongs to whichever piece is at the block's BOTTOM edge: `scrollShadow={false}` (already the Lead Details header's flag, now on the L1 header too and set by Qanvast Ops) hands it to the strip, and a header with no strip under it keeps it. One rule, both header components, every page. | mock |

| REG-373 | Lead Details | **The seam between the header and the tab strip paints again** (user 2026-09-07, found by a 24-agent audit against 1186:2554 / 1186:2813 and confirmed by five independent verifiers). The 1px the mock draws INSIDE the block had never rendered at ≤767, for two compounding reasons: the base rule is `position: sticky; top: 8px; z-index: 20`, so switching to `relative` (REG-301) left the 8px behind as a downward PAINT offset — the header drew 8-124 against a layout box of 0-116 and its stroke landed inside the strip — and REG-368 dropped the z-index to stop the header painting over the stuck strip, which also dissolved its stacking context, so the `z-index: -1` plates escaped to the root and hid behind the page body's white background. Restoring a stacking context (`isolation` / `z-index: 0`) fixes both but traps any overlay rendered INSIDE the header under the strip — the Tooltip is `position: fixed` yet renders inline. So the header paints its own background and its own bottom border at ≤767 and the plates step aside: they exist for the desktop's rounded sticky treatment, and a phone has neither radius nor canvas gutter for them to cover. Measured: header 0→117 flush against the strip, `1px rgb(229,229,229)`, no shadow, and the strip still pinned at 0 with a tab at its top edge — REG-368 holds. The same audit found the Move stage step's From System / Upload row 49px instead of 48 (it is a `<button>`, and Chrome's UA `padding-block: 1px` left its ink a pixel off the row's bottom edge): an explicit `padding-bottom: 0`, the enabled tab keeping its 16 on specificity. And the Qanvast ghost loader drew level-1 on BOTH its header and its strip — the doubled shadow REG-369 removed from the live page — so the ghost's header now drops its own when a strip follows it. | bug fix |

Two more the critic raised are deliberate and unchanged: a block's bottom edge lifts on level-1 INSTEAD of the hairline (REG-298, and three verifiers refuted the claim that it should carry both), and the imports header's desktop-only lack of a stroke, which is outside the mobile question asked. Maths (lib/quotes/math.ts): Unit Price = Cost + Markup · line = Qty × Unit Price × (1 − Discount%) · Subtotal excludes Declined lines · Additional Discount % or S$ · GST per line on the remainder when the switch is on · Margin = (net − cost) / net. Behaviours built beyond the drawn frames, per the reference screenshots and the owner: View by Category / Room / Section with subtotal header rows, Uncategorized / Unassigned buckets, Add Category / Add Section, Collapse All; density Single / Double / Triple = description clamped to 1 / 2 / 3 lines (rows 72 / 80 / 96); the hovered-row kebab (Save to Your Library · Duplicate · Copy Name · Group n Item(s) · Show Full Content · Add Item › · Move Item › · Choose New Group › · Remove — submenus drill down in place with a ‹ row back); bulk bar Group · Hide from client · Delete; drag reorder among siblings by the handle; the thumbnail menu (Upload Image → the attach-files modal in image mode, Use Icon → the five kinds, Upload Logo coming soon); column settings drawer with drag reorder (columns the table cannot draw yet answer coming soon when switched on; a Section column joins the catalogue for the Section view); undo / redo replay whole snapshots; Create ▾ = New Item · New Group · From My Items; **Round Prices** = an S$ additional discount that lands the total on the hundred below (invented behaviour); Expand (toolbar), View Price Book, Export As, Add column → coming soon. The opener's scroll is now let through; a real scroll after that still closes it. Surfaced by the quote editor's row menus; applies to every TextField select. | bug fix |
| REG-417 | Quotes | **Items section: the 29 findings of the adversarial review, fixed** (review 2026-09-11, four lenses — spec · logic · React · responsive — two refuters per finding, none refuted). **Spec**: the MARKUP box shares its cell with "(x%)" and was squeezed into "S$60…" on most lines — the money input now sizes to its figure (`field-sizing: content`, `size` as the fallback) and the percent drops under it when the two do not share the line; the active row's QTY box had 15px for its digits ("6." for 681) — the QTY / UNIT / ROOM / DISCOUNT / GST cells give their 36px controls the room at p8 (the mock widens UNIT to 183 and ROOM to 159 in a frame of its own; a table cannot grow one row's cells without breaking every other row's alignment, so the columns stay put — deviation); the floating "Expand" was cut in half by the ITEM cell's overflow (the active row lifts above its neighbour); the group chevron sits 20 from the edge; the column settings' ITEM DETAILS switches saved but changed nothing — Show item description / images / autonumber now hide the description line and Expand, the thumbnail, and the # column; the panel's two lists share one 36px pitch; Issuing Entity and Add Item are the DS field at **36** (`TextField size="s"`, no raw `<input>` left); the Add Item popover opens on **typing** (spec 03), Create New Item is always live (a blank "Item" line when nothing is typed), "My Items (n)" counts what the filter shows. **Logic**: a queued 600 ms cell write is settled BEFORE any structural write reloads the lines (it used to snap back on screen and land on the server unseen), dropped for lines being removed, and forgotten whole by undo / redo (the snapshot already says what the lines should be); **a group's answer is its children's** — the chip sits on the group row and a child's STATUS cell is empty (spec 03), so the server cascades a group's status to its lines, a line joining a group takes the group's, grouping takes the lines' shared answer, and `countedLines` leaves out everything under a Declined group (a Declined group's own row sums what the client passed on) — the demo's Declined line moves to a top-level item on OM-10008 (repair R23, two new checks); undo / redo's replace validates every row first and runs delete + inserts in one transaction (REG-286); Round Prices refuses a total under S$100 ("Total is under S$100 — nothing to round") instead of discounting the quote to S$0.00, and says when it moves a % discount to S$; a unit price can no longer go negative (the markup is bounded by the cost, "−" leaves the money filter); the additional discount is stored as the value applied (% ≤ 100, S$ ≤ subtotal) and the card shows that; drag reorder stamps the quote and reloads the lines if the write fails. **React**: Escape in a cell discarded the draft on screen but `blur()` committed it (a `cancelled` ref, read live); Upload Logo toggled Show Full Content — it answers coming soon; the table carries `columnheader` / `cell` roles, the Add Item row is a row, its field a combobox (aria-expanded / controls / activedescendant, ArrowUp / Down walk the matches, Enter picks); drill-down menus move focus onto the new level's first row and the icon tiles are menu rows; every radio-style menu (status, View by, density, visibility, unit, label, discount unit) says which value is current (`DropdownItem role="menuitemradio" checked`); the thumbnail, eye, bucket fold and column handle carry the Tooltip, and ArrowUp / Down on a column's handle reorders it. **Responsive**: every Items menu is a bottom sheet at ≤767 (`PortalMenu sheetTitle`, opt-in — the Add Item combobox and TextField selects stay anchored to their field); the section toolbar is ONE row at ≤1024 (the AI mark, Create as its icon, a kebab folding Create Group · Undo · Redo · Expand · Column settings · Row density › · View by › · Collapse All — REG-354 / REG-384, via `useMediaQuery`); 20px marks take 44 boxes (−12), the drag handle and the row "+" step aside on touch (HTML5 drag never starts from a finger; Add Item › Below is the same action) so the selection cell is the checkbox alone; the cell inputs and selects stand 44 tall under a finger; ≤1024 the selection · # · ITEM block freezes on the left (ITEM narrowed to 240) with a shadow once panned; the Add Item content sticks to the left edge at any offset. **Open on the phone**: at 390 nothing is frozen (a frozen 352 beside a frozen 140 leaves no window) and the lines stay a sideways scroller — an interim; proposed to the owner: the Quotes-tab card of REG-377 (thumb · name · status · qty × unit price · total · kebab). Found while verifying: the status chip's menu read its glyph class from the wrong stylesheet, so the current answer was never tinted (fixed). Verified: 37 → 62 checks green at 1440 / 768 touch / 390 touch. | bug fix · user-directed |
| REG-418 | Quotes | **Payment Schedule section built** (1238:29290 · base 1271:37370 · edit 1271:37609 · over-balance 1271:38240 · hover 1271:38624 · banner 1271:39003 / 39197 · loading 1238:29277…; spec 06; owner 2026-09-11; frame ids corrected 2026-09-15). **Data**: `quote_payments` (position · name · description · pct · due kind + date); **amounts are never stored** — every one is the quote's LIVE total × the row's share (REG-403; the mock's S$10,000 rows are placeholders), and when the shares make exactly 100 % the last row absorbs the cent rounding so the amounts sum to the total. Every quote starts on the studio's default split (`createQuote` seeds it; repair R24 gives the seven existing quotes theirs, dated from their own created_at) — the toolbar's "Default: 10/30/30/30" is that state, read back from the rows. **Presets** (owner: "10/30/30/30 + a few options"): Default 10/30/30/30 (the Won wizard's own split, lib/leads/stage-data.ts) · 20/40/40 · 30/70 · 50/50 — invented set; the toolbar names the preset the rows ARE, or "Custom" once anything material changed; picking one replaces the rows with an Undo snackbar. **Due dates**: the deposit is "On Approval"; every other milestone is an ESTIMATE ("~ 21 Oct ’26", the mock's tilde) derived from records — works start on the lead's `estimated_start_date` (else three weeks after the quote was written) and run the property's renovation length (HDB 3-Room 8 weeks · 4-Room / BTO 4-Room / Condo 2-bedder 10 · 5-Room / BTO 5-Room / EC / Condo 3-bedder 12 · Landed 20 — invented trade rule), the milestones spread evenly from start to handover; a date picked in the DatePicker is FIXED and loses the tilde. **Inline edit** (1271:37609): name · description · % · amount become the 2px primary box with ✕ / ✓ under the cell's right edge; Enter and ✓ save, Esc and ✕ discard, blur saves; editing an amount writes the share it implies (amounts stay derived); a % or amount being typed previews at once, so the Total row and the over-balance error react while typing. **Over-balance** (1271:38624): shares past 100 % are writable (the table shows the state, it does not refuse it) — the Total's % and amount turn red and the message reads "Your upcoming scheduled payments are S$X above the proposal’s balance of S$Y" (the mock's own wording, 1271:38240 — re-read 2026-09-15: it reads "proposal’s", not "proposals"; the figures are whole dollars, 14/500). **Rows**: drag handle (motion Reorder — pointer drag, so a finger reorders too), hover wash + the red delete at the row's right (1271:39003) with an Undo snackbar; "Add a Payment" appends "New payment" at 0 % due at handover and opens its name. **Add Payments with AI** (owner: derived from the quote's own items): the three loading cards (700 ms each — invented pacing) then a schedule from the lines — a 10 % deposit on approval, one payment per SECTION of works in the order the lines run (a line's section, else its category), each sized to that section's share of the total (whole percentages, the largest section absorbs the rounding), and a 10 % completion at handover; the banner "Here’s a Maison AI generated payment schedule" (copy per owner) with Undo · ✕, the AI button resting while it shows. **Activity** (REG-343): the lead's feed gains "You added n items to the quote …" (REG-415's lines — missed in phase 2, added here) and "You set the payment schedule of the quote … to 10/30/30/30", both linking to the editor. **Responsive**: 768–1024 the six columns tighten so the table fits the centre without a scroller; ≤767 each milestone is a card (name + description left; share, amount, due stacked right; the handle down the left edge, the delete pinned top-right) and the toolbar folds to the AI mark + a kebab whose sheet holds "Preset ›" and "Add a Payment" (REG-354); | REG-427 | Cross · Quotes | **Resizable columns on every table; the Items table fits its columns to their content and freezes its left block** (owner 2026-09-15: "table columns to be resizable by the user like how it is in the excel sheet across the tables in the prototype" · "columns width be content width (except the name and description) in the quote items table so that it doesn't show in two lines and while editing it turn in single there is some UI jerk" · "Till the item column it is fixed"). **Resize** (`components/om/column-resize.tsx`, `lib/table-widths.ts`): every head cell carries an 8px `col-resize` strip on its right edge with a 2px primary guide on hover / focus / drag (the sheet's rule between two heads); drag sets the width live, release stores it under `om.cols.<table>` in localStorage — the person's preference, like the listing's view, cleared by Reset prototype; double-click (or Home) forgets it (the sheet's auto-fit); ArrowLeft / Right step 8, Shift 32; `role="separator"`. Tables and their keys: leads listing (`leads`, a dragged column stops growing pro rata), Tasks (`lead-tasks`), Quotes tab + Tools › Quotes (`lead-quotes`), Contracts (`lead-contracts`), Files list (`lead-files`), Deleted files (`deleted-files`), Deleted leads (`deleted-leads`), Qanvast Ops (`qanvast-leads`), Import history (`imports`), the quote's Items (`quote-items`) and Payment Schedule (`quote-payments`). Grid tables read `--tracks` (their template with the dragged tracks in px), flex tables `--cw-<key>`; each stylesheet keeps the mocked template as the fallback, so an untouched table is byte-for-byte the mock. Phone card layouts are media rules that never read the variables, so a width dragged on the desktop never reaches the phone. Not resizable: the import wizard's mapping preview and the review sheet's merge table (wizard steps, not working tables). **Items content-fit**: every column but ITEM is as wide as its widest text — the head's label or any row's value, measured in the cell's own font (canvas, re-measured once the webfont has loaded) — plus 36 (the cell's padding, the input's border, 2 of slack), floor 56, so "S$3,185.00 (32.29%)" sits on one line and the active row's boxes change nothing about the height (the wrap under the amount of REG-417 now only happens when the user narrows the column); a dragged width wins over the fit; ITEM keeps 360 (240 ≤1024) and the mocked widths remain the SSR / first-paint fallback (spec 03's 65 · 127 · 115 · 150 · 125 · 101 · 79 are floors no longer — deviation from the mock's frame at the owner's request). **Frozen left**: the selection · # · ITEM block is sticky at every width (REG-417 had it ≤1024 only), the sheet's freeze panes: the line's name stays while the number columns pan under the frozen Total. Now the row's single track is its own height (`grid-auto-rows: 100%`) and cells have `min-height: 0`, so a 64 row holds 64px cells (a 1px content overflow stays inside the cell's padding). The corners: nothing clips a resting card (the row menus hang below a short table), so the frozen cells' square white fill covered the inside of each corner's curve. A ring the size of the border box (`::after`, z 3, pointer-events none) redraws the stroke above the children on Quotes, Files, Contracts and Tasks; once a frame scrolls it clips its own corners and the ring steps aside; the last row and its frozen first cell take the card's inner radius so a hover fill follows the curve; the phone cards drop the ring with the border. Qanvast, Deleted files and Imports already clip. Verified at 3× crops of every corner and the bottom edge. | bug fix |

Verified by measuring every cell's text start before and after activation. | bug fix |

| REG-454 | Tables | **Column settings on every table** (owner 2026-09-16, after an ideation report on the listing's mock popover vs the quote editor's drawer; the owner's decisions: "1. keep its fixed selection 2. Ignore mock and do it as we did in the items list 3. Yes 4. your list"). One drawer — `components/om/column-settings.tsx`, the Items table's design (REG-415: "Column settings" title, helper line, a **Pinned (3 columns max)** group with the identity column locked, **Manage columns** with a drag handle · pin · "Show X column" switch, footer Reset to default · Save) — now serves the leads listing, Lead Details › Quotes and Tools › Quotes, Contracts, Files (list view only), Tasks, Qanvast Ops › Leads and the quote editor's Items. It replaces the listing's mock Customise popover (730:5967, REG-24…) and is entered from a `tune` icon button right after Filters in every list toolbar (the listing's head gear is gone; Qanvast's coming-soon gear too). Preferences (order · visibility · pins) are kept per browser in localStorage `om.columns.<table>` (`lib/column-prefs.ts`) — "Reset to default" also drops dragged widths, and REG-430 stands: widths themselves are never persisted. The selection column is always frozen first, up to three columns pin after it in the drawer's order, the LAST pinned one carries the 1px edge at rest and the shadow while scrolled, and the offsets are measured from the head row (`components/om/table-columns.ts`), so a pinned column follows a dragged or fluid title. At ≤767 the cards ignore the preferences and the button is hidden. Verified: the listing + Items (probe-columns 10/10), the six tables (probe-columns2 68/68: button after Filters, locked identity, hide, pin → sticky at the measured left, order, reload, Reset, no button on a phone), a pinned column mid-scroll on a 1025 desktop (probe-pinscroll 15/15), and the table suites (resize 32, actions 19, titles 7, hover 10, items 56). The task remembers its quote (`lead_tasks.quote_id`, migration 0021), so there is never a second; the snackbar reads "Due date set — follow-up task added" with a **View** action that opens the lead's Tasks tab (owner 2026-09-16: "toast should have view cta which leads to tasks"). | REG-475 | Quote editor | **The Items table is a keyboard grid — the Excel benchmark's phase 1** (owner 2026-09-17: "our users are using excel sheet more and they expect the same to be done in our quote editor as well … do a usability benchmark and give me a report of what are missing from an excel use case pov", then "sounds good go ahead" on the report's phases). The benchmark found the table already had the spreadsheet's structure — in-place cells, undo, autonumber, subtotals, frozen and resizable columns, insert / move / duplicate, bulk actions — but not its keyboard or its clipboard: Tab left the cell for the row's next button, Enter dropped the focus on the page, the arrows stayed in the box, Shift-click ticked one row, no Cmd+Z, no paste. Phase 1, built: Tab / Shift+Tab step across a row's cells and wrap to the next / previous row; Enter / Shift+Enter and ↑ / ↓ walk one column, skipping a row with no cell in it (a group has no QTY), past the last row into the Add Item field; ← / → leave a box only from its edge or with the value selected; a cell reached by key opens with its value selected so typing replaces it, F2 drops the caret at the end; Space opens a select, Backspace / Delete clears it; Cmd+Z / Cmd+Shift+Z (Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y) undo and redo the lines from the page body or anywhere in the card — a box with typing in it keeps the browser's own undo, other dialogs and menus their own keys; Shift-click selects a run of rows, as the Tasks table does. A click still puts the caret where it lands (the mock's cell, REG-417). Phases 2 and 3 followed the same day (REG-476 / REG-477). `components/quotes/editor/items/grid-keys.ts`; probe `probe-grid.cjs` (33 checks); `design-source/verify/quote-editor/items-grid-keys.png`. | user-directed |
| REG-476 | Quote editor | **Paste from a spreadsheet, copy rows out, a real export — the Excel benchmark's phase 2** (owner 2026-09-17: "go ahead" on the benchmark's phases). A block copied from Excel / Sheets / Numbers — tabs between cells, a line per row — pasted anywhere in the Items table or into the Add Item field opens *Paste from a spreadsheet* (L 800, no mock, `paste-items-dialog.tsx`): each column picks the field it becomes; a header row is read for the first guess (Item · Qty · Unit · Rate → Cost …), without one the values' shapes are (text → Item then Description, unit words → Unit, a whole-number column → Qty, the next → Cost, a column that multiplies qty × cost is the sheet's amount and is skipped — totals are derived here); the first six rows preview under the picks; "First row is a header"; Add "After {row}" (into the group when that row is one) or "At the end"; *Add n items* writes the lines in one transaction (`createQuoteItems`), undone by one Cmd+Z. S$ and commas parse, a markup typed "20%" is that share of the cost, a lone Unit Price is the cost with no markup, so the totals still read right. A single value pastes into its box as ever. Out: the bulk bar's **Copy** and **Cmd+C** (rows ticked, no box focused) put the rows on the clipboard as tab-separated text under a header — # · Item · Description · Qty · Unit · Cost · Markup · Unit Price · Discount · GST · Room · Category · Section · Total — so they paste straight into a sheet; **Export As** on the Add Item row exports every line in table order with the groups' sums, named `{OM-number}-items` — first as a two-item menu (Excel · CSV, with an in-house .xlsx writer), the same day through the house Export dialog and its writer instead (REG-478). `lib/quotes/clipboard.ts` holds the parser, the guesses and the row shapes. | user-directed |
| REG-477 | Quote editor | **Drag-fill, Cmd+D, Set value, "=" sums, a sum row, sort by head, Find — the Excel benchmark's phase 3** (same day). *Drag-fill*: the small square at a focused box's corner (Excel's fill handle) drags down or up over rows, outlining the cells it will fill, and fills them on release — a cost keeps each line's markup in bounds, a unit price becomes each line's own markup, a label lands on groups too; **Cmd+D** fills the focused cell's value into the ticked rows, or — none ticked — copies the cell above into it (Excel's Fill Down); one Cmd+Z takes a fill back whole. The bulk bar's **Set value** (the form dialog, no mock, `set-value-dialog.tsx`): one field (Qty · Unit · Cost · Markup % · Unit Price · Discount % · GST % · Room · Category · Section) and one value for every ticked line, a markup as a share of each line's own cost. A number box takes a **sum** — "=12*3.5", "=1200/4", "=20%*300" — worked out on commit by a small recursive-descent parser (`lib/quotes/arith.ts`, never `eval`); one that does not work out leaves the value. A **sum row** under the lines totals Qty, Cost, Markup, Unit Price and Total over the lines that price (its Total is the card's subtotal); it is measured into the content-fit. **Sort by head** on Item, Qty, Cost, Markup, Unit Price and Total was built here — a press wrote the order, a second turned it round — and removed the same day (REG-483). **Find**: a search glyph in the toolbar opens a 240 box (Escape clears it) that narrows the rows to the names and descriptions carrying the text, each under its group and bucket; the sum row keeps the whole quote. Found on the way: a reload after a structural write or an undo landed a beat later and hid a value typed in that beat — every reload now merges the cell writes still queued over the stored rows, and a reload another has overtaken is dropped. Not copied from Excel: cross-cell formulas, merged cells, free extra columns. Probe `probe-excel2.cjs` (42 checks, 1440); `design-source/verify/quote-editor/items-paste-dialog.png` · `items-excel-phase3.png`. | user-directed |
| REG-478 | Quote editor | **Expand is the icon alone under the description box; Export As is the house Export dialog** (owner 2026-09-17: "this expand can be just the icon like what we did for ✕ / ✓ which is right aligned" · "Export as we have a model in the lead export flow were we ask for the format let it be consistent like the lead flow"). The active row's Expand chip — icon + word, hanging left under the row (1271:40880) — is now the icon in a 24px box 2 under the description box's right end, seated as the ✕ / ✓ pair is (REG-472), tooltip "Expand", label "Expand description"; it still opens the S 400 description modal (REG-444). Export As on the Add Item row opens the Export dialog the leads listing and the Tasks tab use (784:113420 / 902:29293: "Choose file format", CSV · XLS · XLSX · PDF, one writer in `lib/leads/export.ts`) titled "Export Items", in place of the two-item menu REG-476 added the same day; the file is `{OM-number}-items.{ext}`, money with two decimals, the snackbar "Exported n items as XLSX · name" as the Tasks export's. The in-house `lib/quotes/xlsx.ts` from REG-476 is gone — the leads' writer is the one. | user-directed |
| REG-479 | Quote editor | **Round Prices is the "Round selling prices" dialog** (owner 2026-09-17: "round prices is not working i guess i had given a reference for you to build like the first screenshot" — the reference: title *Round selling prices*, NEAREST 1 · 5 · 10 · 50 · 100, Nearest · Round up · Round down, "No line prices would change at this step. 3 skipped (no chargeable price).", a preview box, Cancel · Apply). | REG-485 | Templates | **Quote templates: the record, the gallery, the lifecycle** (owner 2026-09-17: "users create, customise, and publish the quotes templates globally at their studio"). A template is design + content defaults (`lib/templates/model.ts` — page, brand overrides, blocks; `TemplateDefaults` — payment preset, T&Cs and notes templates, validity, GST, client signature, intro). `quote_templates` gained status (draft · published · archived), version, the published `design_json` / `defaults_json`, the working `draft_json`, `source_file`, `note`; `quote_template_versions` keeps every publish (migration 0022). Lifecycle: Draft → Published vN → Default. **Publish** (`publishTemplate`) makes the draft version n+1 with a note, optionally sets the studio default and moves open Draft quotes to the version; **sent quotes keep the version they were sent with** (the quote pins `template_version`); Roll back publishes an old version again as a new one; Discard drops a published template's draft or deletes a never-published one. Owners and Admins edit and publish — no review step (owner: "yes"); comments stay coming soon. The gallery (`components/company/templates/quote-templates-gallery.tsx`): filter All · Published · Drafts · Archived with counts, search, cards drawing each template's **real first page** (`TemplateThumb`), name · Default tag · "Published v1 · 2d ago by Dane" · "Used by n quotes" · paper, a kebab (Edit · Set as default · Duplicate · Rename · Archive/Restore · Delete — the default can't be archived or deleted, a draft can't be archived), New template ▾ (Blank · From a house style… · Recreate from my current quote…) and Upload my current quote. The three seeded templates keep their mock names and wear the house styles (repair R31). | user-directed |
| REG-486 | Templates | **The template editor** (owner 2026-09-17: "a rich canva/doc kind of interface … match pixel perfect"; UI from the Claude Design artifact's screens 5a–5d and 6a–6d only, corrected: no settings nav, no review, no authored Pages tab, inspector = five quick controls + More options). `/templates/[id]` in the rail-less `(editor)` group (`components/templates/editor/*`): a 56 top bar (back · the name typed in place · Draft / Published vN / Default / Unpublished changes chips · the autosave stamp · Preview with ▾ (Sample quote · Empty quote · any recent quote) · zoom · undo / redo · History · ⋯ · Preview · Publish), **Structure · Brand · Defaults** on the left (300), the paginated document in the middle, the inspector on the right (320). **Hybrid document** (`components/templates/render/*`): header, footer and cover are **canvases** — elements in mm (text, image, details rows, rule, box, signature columns, badges, page number) that select, drag, resize and type in place, with Every page / First page / After the first page repeat rules; the body **blocks flow and paginate live** (`document.tsx` measures every slice once in a hidden box the width of the page body and packs pages under the header and over the footer; keep-with-next, keep a section together, a repeated table head on continuation pages, page totals, empty rows to the foot, a cover as its own page, a watermark, a "Powered by OpenMaison" line). Structure: page setup (A4 · Letter, margins, watermark), the outline with cover and header pinned, the flow reordered by its handle, the footer pinned, "Page n" markers where the live pagination breaks, an eye per block, Add block (singletons once). Inspector: per block kind its five controls first — Items: Group by · Then by · Section labels · Item numbers · Section bands, then the columns list; Totals: lines · layout · width · rules; and so on — then More options (every other setting, the text styles, start on a new page, keep with next, space before/after). **Cmd+Z / Shift+Cmd+Z** over everything (typing coalesces into one step, a drag lands as one); Delete removes the selection; Esc clears it. The draft **autosaves** 900ms after the last change ("Saved just now"). Publish dialog (6d corrected): what changed · make it the studio's default · move open draft quotes to this version, "sent quotes keep the version they were sent with". History drawer lists every version with its note and Roll back. Tablets fold the panes into Structure / Inspector drawers; phones view only (REG-493). Verified: verify-templates.cjs (editor, canvas, undo, publish, history). | user-directed |
| REG-487 | Templates | **Brand kit and house styles** (owner 2026-09-17: "Yes you please find a real quotes and design them … they want to match pixel perfect of their current quote"). The **brand kit** (`settings` key `brand`, `lib/templates/brand.ts`, repair R31): logo, dark logo, stamp (a generated one until uploaded, `lib/templates/assets.ts`), accreditation badges, six named colours (Brand ink · Brand grey · Accent · Brand light · Band · Highlight) and four type roles (Heading · Body · Table · Small print, from the curated font list — Arial, Helvetica, Times New Roman, Georgia, Courier New, Calibri, Cambria, Verdana, Trebuchet MS, Inter, Montserrat, Lato, Playfair Display, Fraunces), plus the company details every template prints ({studio_name}, {studio_uen}, {studio_address}, …). Edited under **Company › Profile** (`components/company/profile-view.tsx`: Logo and marks · Colours · Type · Company details, the default template's first page redrawing beside the form; Save writes the kit and keeps the Settings studio record's name and phone in step). A template's **Brand tab** shows the kit's values and marks what this template overrides, with Reset to brand per value and for everything. **House styles** (`lib/templates/presets.ts`) rebuild the owner's three reference quotes (design-source/reference/quotes/README.md): **Letter** (Dyel's Sales Contract: the letter block on every page, lettered trades › rooms, amount-only column, Inclusive, two signatures in the footer), **Scope** (Flo Design: particulars in two columns, lettered sections with sub-totals, red unit column, FOC, badges strip), **Form** (Cove Interior: Letter paper, a boxed grid with a total per page and empty rows to the foot, three signature columns with the stamp), plus **Presentation** (a cover, rooms with photos, an investment summary, payment cards). "From a house style" starts a template from one; the seeded templates wear the first three. Every house style is drawn with the studio's own kit and the sample quote, so the cards show what the studio would get. | user-directed |
| REG-488 | Templates | **The Items block's options** — what the reference quotes needed (REG-487's analysis; owner: "match pixel perfect"). Group by the quote's own groups, Trade, Room or Section, then by Room or Trade; section labels A · B · C / 1 · 2 · 3 / none; item numbers restarting per section, per room, running through, or none; section bands grey / accent / black / underlined / inside the description column; notes under the bands (the group's description); header row plain / ruled / grey / black / none; descriptions hidden / first line / every line / bullets; photos; sub-total per section with its label; **lump sum** per section (one amount instead of a price per line); zero amounts as FOC / Inclusive / 0.00 / blank; currency S$ / "$ " / figures only; grid lines, a rule under each row, zebra rows; row height; **fill the page with empty rows**; **a total for each page** with its label; keep a section on one page; each section on a new page; hidden items left out or shown without a price; an **Optional works** section (REG-492) with its title; the text styles of rows, header, section and room labels, section notes and descriptions. Columns: #, Item, Qty, Unit, Qty · Unit (merged), Unit price, Discount, Total — each with its own label, width (mm) and alignment; the Item column fills. | user-directed |
| REG-489 | Quote editor | **Template touchpoints and version pinning** (owner 2026-09-17: the build order's second step). **Create Quote** modal: only published templates, each tile drawing the template's real first page with the studio's kit (the placeholder document is gone), "vN · used 2w ago"; View all and New design template open Company › Templates › Quotes; Preview opens the template editor read-only in a new tab. **createQuote** applies the template's Defaults tab — payment split, T&Cs and notes templates (tokens resolved), validity, GST, client signature — and pins `template_version`. **General Info** grew a fourth row under the mock's three: the **Template** select (the published templates, or "No template — the studio default"), "Pinned to v1 · v2 is published", Edit template (the editor in a new tab) and Save as new template (a Draft template from this quote's settings; snackbar "Open"). Under the tab strip, **the update banner**: "{template} was updated to v2 — {note}. This quote still prints with v1." · Keep v1 · Update to v2 (one click moves the quote to the latest version; sent quotes stay pinned to what they were sent with). The header's Preview, Download as PDF and Print open the Preview (REG-490); Save as Template opens its dialog; Rename, Duplicate, Export, Version History, Comments and Delete still answer coming soon. | user-directed |
| REG-490 | Quote editor | **Preview: what the client sees, exactly** (owner 2026-09-17: "What you see is what you get is whatever is related for client only those will be shown to them … the quote format"). `/quotes/[id]/preview` (`components/quotes/preview/quote-preview.tsx`): the quote through its template at the pinned version, paginated as the PDF and the portal will render it (`lib/templates/queries.ts fetchQuotePreview`). Above the pages, **readiness** (`lib/templates/readiness.ts`; the Houzz "don't forget" idea): no items to price, no client name, no valid-until, nobody as Prepared by, no site address where the template prints one, a payment schedule that doesn't add up to 100%, empty T&Cs / notes the template prints, your signature not set up — blocking issues disable Send, each with Open (the editor at that section). **What your client sees** panel: the per-quote switches over the template's own view — columns, descriptions, photos, hidden items (left out / shown without a price), optional works, subtotal breakdown, payment schedule, T&Cs, notes, signature, the "Powered by OpenMaison" line — autosaved as the difference from the template (`quotes.client_view_json`), counted on the button, Reset to template. **Internal copy** adds an "Internal copy" watermark and a cost · selling · margin · lines card the client never sees; cost and markup never print. Copy link, **Download PDF** = the same pages printed (`@page` at the template's paper, the chrome hidden; `?print=1` prints on arrival — the header's Download as PDF and Print), Send (the editor's action). | user-directed |
| REG-491 | Templates | **Recreate from my current quote** (owner 2026-09-17: "Currently, the users have their own templates designed using some other softwares and which we need to match as well"). New template ▾ › Recreate… / Upload my current quote (`components/company/templates/recreate-dialog.tsx`): a real upload (PDF · JPG · PNG, dropped or browsed), a reading beat, then the match — the house style that reproduces it with the reasons ("A letter: the studio's block … repeat on every page"), "Choose another style", a name — and the editor opens with the file **beside the canvas** (the right pane's Reference tab: a PDF in its viewer, a photo or scan as a picture that can also **lie under the first page** at a chosen strength to line the blocks up). The match is **curated** for the owner's three reference quotes (Sharon's / Dyel → Letter, Flo → Scope, Stanley / Cove → Form; presentation, grid and table words steer the rest; anything else lands on Letter) — the Maison AI reading is a later build and is not claimed. The file is kept with the template (`source_file`, up to 6 MB inline; larger files keep their name) and can be removed. | REG-524 | Templates | **Company › Templates audit fixes** (2026-09-18): the Profile's colour and size fields can be typed (drafts commit on a valid value, with the DS error state), unsaved edits warn on unload, the badge's Remove is on where a finger cannot hover, saving no longer renders twice; Restore / Delete Forever / Empty Trash are single actions (`restoreTemplates`, `deleteTemplatesForever`, `emptyTemplateTrash`) with the gallery's own in-use predicate (live, unarchived quotes) — no partial purges; the rail's Company button names its awaiting count; the in-use Delete Forever and a fully in-use Empty Trash keep their reason reachable (aria-disabled, tooltip); the Awaiting approval filter shows only while the setting requires approval or something waits; Rename / Delete keep their name through the exit frame (the Files tab's Shown pattern); every way into the editor carries its origin; the card's status line and the row's Updated column read one stamp; the row menu measures six rows, not seven; the list's touch rules match the Files list and reserve the kebab's band on whichever column is last; the ready-made design dialog opens clean every time and shows two cards across on a tablet; dead props, the archived card rule, `data-status` on rows, an unused export and the Profile / tab-count ghost drift removed. | bug fix |
| REG-525 | Style | **Style, motion and responsive pass** (2026-09-18): `--motion-modal` exists at last (nine stylesheets asked for it through a fallback); the Items table's frozen shadows are the tokens and the frozen ITEM column fades its shadow; the template editor's chrome (selection outlines, handles) reads the colour tokens; `100vh` → `100dvh` in the Create Quote modal, the Deleted sheets and the picker; the Create Quote picker's footer takes the sheet's 16 gutter and clears the home indicator; raw 2px radii are `--om-radius-0-5`; payment rows fade and every hover fill is gated to a hovering pointer (cards, tiles, deleted sheets, the templates list); the template editor's name field rings on keyboard focus only, its bar has no stray z-index, its spinner respects reduced motion, its hovers fade; the Structure / Inspector / Brand panes take 44 boxes on touch; a section card's head stays 68 open or closed; Round prices matches the house eyebrow tracking and frame stroke; Activity's spacing is tokens; the Preview's "What your client sees" is the house Drawer below 1025 (it was a hand-rolled fixed column); dead CSS removed. | bug fix |
| REG-526 | Audit | **Deep audit backlog** (2026-09-18) — found, logged, not built: the Items table's hover wash is a gradient overlay and cannot fade (a colour wash would drop the band / selected fills); Blank Quote in the picker starts at once while a template selects then confirms; the payment Undo can race a slow delete; Change template applies the new grouping only when groups exist; the Activity "sent" line reads the current status; native `title` tooltips in the Items head and menus; the Items table unfreezes every column ≤767 (REG-417 interim); the quote editor's client pane and the template editor's two panes leave a narrow canvas at 1025–1200; the three Deleted sheets and the two tile modules could share one stylesheet each; pill padding, empty-state padding and the remaining raw spacing across the editor stylesheets; the template editor's pane strip is its own tabs (om Tabs is routed); canvas Escape commits while the name field reverts, and there is no touch way into in-place canvas text; the inspector's select matches by label; multiline fields lose their label once filled; an image block is measured before it loads; a local publish shows a blank avatar until reload; the quote editor's Send has no readiness gate (Preview's does); the Maison AI store is last-writer-wins (latent); the Company and Tools popovers duplicate their placement code; the kind pages' "Coming soon" sentence is built from each kind's help line (invented copy, kept); the Preview's template chip is hand-rolled. | user-directed |
| REG-527 | Quotes | **Edge add on the Items rows** (owner 2026-09-18: "At the top & end of the row when mouse is hovered user added a + button throughout the row so that they can easily add a row on top/bottom… mouse tracking is hard so travel to the left"; then "just have the hovering edge adds … at the bottom only and remove the top one"). Hovering a row shows a 22px primary + on its bottom edge, at the pointer's x (it follows the mouse along the row); hovering the + draws a 2px primary line along that edge. It adds a line right below the row (the leading +'s Add item below); a group's adds inside it; Add item above stays in the kebab. Mouse only — a finger keeps the leading + and the kebab. No mock; the Notion / Sheets insertion handle. | user-directed |
| REG-528 | Quotes | **The template picker: Manage Templates and a list view** (owner 2026-09-18: "Add a button Manage Templates that takes to quote templates listing… have list view even in this so that the users can see the names completely"). The Choose a template dialog's list head carries Manage Templates (a text button to Company › Templates › Quotes, with the page as the way back, REG-465) and the Files tab's grid / list toggle (remembered per browser in `om.picker.list`). The list draws 56-tall rows — the first page as a 26×36 glyph, the whole name, the Default chip, "vN · used …" — with the grid's selection ring, double-click to use, and Blank Quote as its first row where a quote is being started. | user-directed |
| REG-529 | Tools | **Quote X-Ray** (owner 2026-09-18: "build this feature based on our design principles and ds… take the feature reference from the 1.0, design and build by yourself in 2.0"). No 2.0 mock — the 1.0 tool's nine screens are the reference, every surface is 2.0's own. **Entry points:** a live Tools tile, the lead's Quote X-Ray quick link (the lead preselected), and the quote editor kebab's Compare with another quote (lead and quote preselected) — each carrying its origin so Back returns (REG-465). **Setup** (`/quote-x-ray`, L1 header): a dismissable intro card (remembered per browser), the DS segmented control For a lead · Upload two quotes, the lead in the select field, the lead's quotes as ChoiceCards (number · title, amount · status · updated), the Move stage Dropzone → FileTile for the other studio's file, the hint and Compare in a footer; Recent comparisons beside it ("{Lead} · vs {Studio}", chevron, delete → "Delete this comparison? … deleted permanently", hard delete). **Reading:** the four-step progress card (about 3 s) then the record. **Record** (`/quote-x-ray/[id]`, L2 header, crumb Quote X-Ray, New comparison): the summary (eyebrow, headline, three tiles — yours · theirs · Real difference — and the footnote), Say this (up to three numbered points with evidence, Say it as a question, Send to client), Key points (brands as advantage / risk cards with impact and sources), Item by item on the listing's table model (Item frozen · Trade · Yours · Theirs · Difference · Status, Column settings, cards ≤767, a status band per row). **Send to client:** the m 600 dialog — the editable WhatsApp draft, Reset to draft, the count, Copy · Open WhatsApp (the lead's number). **Engine:** curated and deterministic (`lib/xray/*`): your side is the quote's real lines; the other studio's file is read by name as one of the three reference quotes (Dyel · Flo Design · Cove Interior) with curated lines; matching by the words, the like-for-like difference, points from the biggest missing scope and prices well below ours, brand notes from a curated table; any other file says "We couldn’t read this one — try a PDF from the client." Stored whole in `quote_comparisons` (migration 0025), on the lead's Activity, seeded by repair R33 (two readings), baseline refreshed. Every sentence is templated — invented copy. | user-directed |
| REG-530 | Tools | **Quote X-Ray under a lead is an L3** (owner 2026-09-18: "when i'm under a lead details > quote x-ray then the lead should be preselected and with only that particular lead and the header should have back like it is in the lead details L2 page with breadcrumbs"). Opened from a lead (the quick link) or from one of its quotes (the editor's kebab), the page wears the L2 header — Back to where it came from, crumb "Leads / {Lead}" (or "Quotes / {number}"), title Quote X-Ray — the lead is shown locked (name + property type) instead of the picker, and Recent comparisons lists only that lead's. The record opened from there reads "{Lead} / Quote X-Ray" in its crumb. From Tools the page keeps its L1 header and every lead. | user-directed |
| REG-531 | Quote editor | **The kebab's "Compare with another quote" was clipped** (owner 2026-09-18: "broken at the bottom"). The header's Dropdown was the mocks' 200px card, the new label wrapped to two lines and the fixed 36px row cut the second one under the next item. The menu is now 264 wide, as the template menus with long labels (the gallery's New template ▾, the editor's Preview ▾); and the shared menu row is `min-height: 36px` instead of a fixed height, so a label that wraps anywhere grows its row instead of being clipped (byte-identical for one-line rows: 8 + 20 + 8 = 36). | bug fix |
| REG-532 | Quote editor | **By category under the Items table** (owner 2026-09-18, from 1.0's "BY CATEGORY" block: "evaluate the best placement… without disturbing what we have built"; proposal `design-source/context/quote-editor/proposals/by-category-placement.png` accepted "go"). Placement: the block below the table is now a grid — Issuing Entity over *By category* on the left, the 434 totals card spanning both rows on the right — so the split reads beside the Subtotal it splits and nothing built moves. Rows = the categories the quote prices (a line's own, else its group's, else Uncategorized — the View by › Category buckets), biggest share first, Uncategorized last: name · 6px primary bar · share of the subtotal (one decimal under 10); a category with no priced line reads "n · no cost". Two columns filled down the left first, one column ≤1024; ≤767 it stacks after the totals card. A row switches View by to Category; when that bucket holds priced lines it opens alone, scrolled into view (the view buckets top-level rows — a mixed group stays whole under Uncategorized — so a share's bucket can be empty, and then the view simply switches). Internal: it does not print in Preview or the client view. Pure maths `categoryShares` in `lib/quotes/math.ts` (counted lines only — Declined and Optional stay out, as in the totals). Copy ("By category · n · share of the subtotal", "n · no cost", the row tooltip) is invented. | user-directed |
| REG-533 | Quote editor | **The Items toolbar ran past the card at 1025** (found verifying REG-532: with "View by: Category" — four characters longer than "None" — the wide toolbar at 1025 overflowed the section by ~144px). The section head's actions had `flex-shrink: 0` at every width, so the toolbar's `flex-wrap` never got the chance; at ≥1025 they may now shrink (`min-width: 0`) and the toolbar folds to a second right-aligned row while the card is too narrow, the head growing from its 68 (≤1024 keeps the folded single row, REG-354). | bug fix |
| REG-534 | Contracts | **Contracts and contract templates built** (owner 2026-09-18: "build contracts and contracts template editors… check all the touchpoints… every ingress working like the quotes"; reference = the Houzz Pro contract screens in Figma 1462:5340 plus the owner's own Contract & Signing exploration; decisions: signing like 1.0 — a link shared on WhatsApp, verified by a one-time code; client only, no vendors / subcontractors; templates as a simple library, no versions or approval; a Singapore interior-design service agreement as the seeded copy). **Editor** `/contracts/[id]` (rail-less): the quote editor's header — crumb "Leads / {lead} / Contracts" · [title ▾] switching the lead's contracts · chips (Signed date · Uploaded PDF · From template) · Saved · [status ▾ Draft · Sent to Client · Signed] · Send · Preview · Automation (reminders / thank-you switches, stored, emails inert) · Copy signing link · kebab (Duplicate and Edit · Save as Template · Download as PDF · Print · Archive · Delete) · ✕ — a meta row (title · recipient chip → Edit recipient · Contract date) — the formatting bar (undo · redo · Text style ▾ Normal / H1 / H2 / H3 · B I U highlight · align ▾ · lists · link · image · Outline toggle · **Smart Text ▾** (two levels: Client · Project · Studio · Quote · Date, each token with its live value) · **eSignatures** drawer with four fillable fields) — the canvas: Outline pane (headings) · the 816 paper (in-house contenteditable grown from the quote's rich editor: chips and signature fields are `contenteditable=false` atoms) · autosave 600 ms. Choosing Signed opens *Mark as signed* (date + optional signed copy). **Send** = *Send for digital signature* (m 600): client name · +65 phone for the code · the WhatsApp draft · the signing link; Copy link · Send via WhatsApp opens wa.me and marks Sent to Client. **Preview** `/contracts/[id]/preview` (quote preview's bar): the brand letterhead, chips resolved, signature boxes; `?print=1` prints (Download as PDF · Print). **Signing page** `/sign/[id]` (no rail): letterhead · the paper · a card walking Send code → the code (any six digits) → type your name → Sign; the name prints as the signature and the contract is Signed. **Templates**: Company › Templates › Contracts live (gallery grid / list on the Quotes gallery's shell, New template ▾ Blank / From the studio agreement, kebab Open · New contract from this template · Duplicate · Rename · Delete, Deleted Templates sheet); the template editor `/templates/contracts/[id]` = the contract editor in library mode (Done · Duplicate · Delete). **Every ingress**: the tab's View / From Blank / From Template (picker with Manage Templates, "Start from the studio agreement") / Import from File (title · PDF · "already signed" switch → Signed with the file as signed copy) / Preview and Share / Copy Link / PDF / Print / Manage Templates / Deleted Contracts; Tools › Contracts `/contracts` (the studio list with a Lead column, New Contract asks the lead first); the Overview's Contract quick link; the quote editor kebab's "Create contract from this quote" (the agreement's Quote chips read that quote); the Won step's Create Contract; Activity rows (created / uploaded · sent · signed online / marked signed · deleted) open the editor. Still coming soon: Create with AI, Contracts Settings. **Data**: contracts + body · recipient · contract_date · template_id · from_quote_id · source · file_name · issuing_entity · reminders · thank_you · signed_by · created_by; table `contract_templates` (migration 0026); repair R34 writes every composed contract's document and the two templates; baseline refreshed. Copy — the agreement's clauses, the Variation Order, the WhatsApp draft, the signing page's lines, the dialogs — is invented. Probe `verify-contracts.cjs` (39). | user-directed |

Tasks, T&Cs & Notes and Payments are galleries now, on one generic piece (`components/company/templates/library-gallery.tsx` = the Quotes gallery's shape: New template ▾ **per tab, creating that tab's kind** + the page kebab's Deleted Templates, segment filters with counts (Tasks: In use · Not used yet; T&Cs: Terms · Notes; Payments: Studio presets · Your own), search, grid / list on the listing's table model with Column settings, rename / duplicate / delete per card, the shared Deleted sheet with Restore · Delete forever · Empty trash). Each kind has a rail-less editor on the contract editor's header (`/templates/tasks/[id]`, `/templates/terms/[id]`, `/templates/payments/[id]`; `components/company/templates/editors/*`): a task template = name + Keep team members / Keep descriptions + reorderable steps (title · description · due in n days · members · tags); a text template = name + the in-house rich editor with Insert Token ▾; a payment template = name + milestone rows (name · description · share) with the running total. Every Manage Templates link (the Tasks tab's ⋯ and its Use Template chooser, the quote editor's My Templates modal, the Quotes / Contracts tabs and Tools lists, the pickers) opens its own gallery **with `?from=`**: the Company pages honour it (`CompanyShell from` → the L2 header's Back + "{origin} / Company" crumbs, the tab strip carries it, an editor opened from there comes back to it). | REG-539 | Quote editor · Templates | **The quote ↔ template touchpoints the audit found broken** (owner 2026-09-23: "fix all of these"). (1) **Change template on a Draft asks "Apply {template}'s defaults to this quote?"** (Apply defaults · Keep mine) after the look changes: the schedule, validity from today, GST, the signature switch, the Terms / Notes templates (only where the template names one), the working columns and — when the table is still empty — the starter sections land; the lines stay; the REG-497 regroup question follows it. Never silent, never on a sent quote. (2) **Save as Template keeps what the quote wrote**: the dialog's "Also save its Terms and Notes as text templates" switch (on) writes them to the T&Cs & Notes library and the draft's Defaults point at them; a split no library template matches becomes a payment template ("{name} split") — the custom split is never lost to `'custom'` again. (3) **The kebab is live**: Rename Quote (a dialog on the title, through General Info's autosave), Duplicate and Edit (a Draft copy — lines, schedule, files, settings, template — with its own number, "(copy)"), Export (the Items export dialog, REG-478), Delete (the tab's dialog, then back where the editor came from); Version History and Comments still answer coming soon. (4) **Create contract from this quote opens the contract template picker** (From Blank · the library · Import stays on the tab) with this quote as the agreement's source. (5) **The live client view pane draws the quote's own client view** — the Preview's overrides and the two "Show in client view" switches — so the pane and Preview never disagree; the Preview's Terms / Notes rows write the same switches. (6) **Only a published template starts or changes a quote** (`createQuote` / `setQuoteTemplate` refuse drafts reached by URL). (7) **Payment Schedule presets ▾ reads the Payments library**: every split by name, "Custom · 15/25/30/30" marked current, "Save this split as a template" (a name dialog; the new template joins the menu at once and the snackbar's Open lands in its editor), "Manage payment templates". (8) The Defaults pane offers the library's splits and the live text templates; its dead "Introduction on every quote" field is gone (the intro is the Title & intro block's own text) and **"Items table opens with"** choice chips set the working columns a new quote's table starts with (`defaults.columns`, written to `itemsColumns`). (9) The picker's facts add **Text** (the Terms / Notes templates a quote opens with) and name a library split. (10) `usedBy` counts live quotes on live leads and attributes template-less quotes to the studio default; the quotes gallery's Deleted sheet shows who deleted (`deleted_by`). | user-directed |

| REG-542 | Quote template editor | **The editor fixed and simplified** (owner 2026-09-23: "quote template editor view looks completely broken … go in depth for every visual, component, section, UI, UX, interaction, transition, accessibility, legibility, responsiveness and functionality … make sure it is super simple for an elderly studio guy"). **Layout.** The editor is an app frame — `position: fixed; inset: 0` with the document's scroll locked while it is mounted — so no portal, overlay or transient layout can push the panes off the top of the window (the owner's screenshot: the panes' bottoms 150px into the viewport, grey below; not reproducible from a fresh load on either server, so the frame removes the class of fault). Below 1200 the two 300 / 320 panes left the page at 43%: the panes become drawers (Structure · Edit over the canvas) from 1199 down, fit-to-width never magnifies past 100%, the name keeps 120px (it vanished at 1025), a tablet's chips wrap under the name instead of clipping mid-word. **Top bar.** Every icon button is 36 (was 24); the source control reads "Preview with: Sample quote" with a tooltip; ⋯ › **Rename template** puts the caret in the name; with nothing to publish the button says **Published** instead of a greyed "Publish changes". **Structure.** "Page setup · A4 · 12/18/10/18 mm" (was "Page · A4, 12/18/10/18 mm"), a Margins caption with Top / Right / Bottom / Left, "List layout" not "list", the eye 36. **Inspector.** Resting state "Nothing selected" with three plain steps (1 pick a part of the page · 2 change its words, columns or look · 3 press Publish); the Items table's Columns and the Totals' lines are one readable row each — switch · the printed words labelled with the column's name · width · alignment — where a label-plus-field pair squeezed "Unit price" to "R…"; selects span the pane unless paired on purpose; paragraph fields keep a visible label; the Items "More options" wall is captioned **Table look · Amounts · Sections and pages · Text styles · Placement** (every block's More options carries Text styles / Placement); the style editor says "Text style" and "Same as the text style" instead of "Role" / "Role font" / "Role weight"; the page-number element lists as "Page number"; a text element carries "Tip: double-click the text on the page to type straight into it"; the head's buttons are 36. **Safety.** Removing a block or an element — from the inspector or with Backspace / Delete — answers with a snackbar and **Undo**. **In-place text** took no focus (React never auto-focuses a div): the editor focuses it with the caret at the end. **Keyboard.** Arrow keys walk the Structure / Brand / Defaults tabs; every block, canvas and element on the page is a Tab stop (Enter selects, Enter again on a text edits) with a dashed focus ring; the resize handle is 13px. **Orientation.** A one-line hint under the top bar on the first visits — "Click any part of the page to change it on the right. Everything is saved as you go — press Publish when it looks right." — dismissed for good with Got it (`om.tpl.hint`). Copy invented. Probe `verify-template-editor.cjs` (28: the frame, the hint, every pane and control above, remove + Undo, keyboard, inline edit, rename, sources and zoom, add block, brand override, preview, publish dialog + discard, history, the origin chip, seven widths incl. the 320 gate); screenshots in `design-source/verify/template-editor/`. | user-directed |
