| REG-664 | Folders — the kit, the galleries, the pickers, the Files tab | **One folder model, one kit, every library** (REG-663). **Data:** `library_folders` (id · library · name · parent_id · created_by · created_at · updated_at · deleted_at; migration 0052, additive) and `folder_id` on `quote_templates` · `contract_templates` · `task_templates` · `quote_text_templates` · `payment_templates` · `price_book_items` · `price_book_sections`; `lib/folders.ts` (pure: `foldersIn` · `trailTo` · `foldersUnder` · `placeOf` · `liveFolder` · `folderNameProblem` · `countLine`; `MSG.folderLength` · `MSG.folderTaken`), `lib/company/folder-libraries.ts` (the seven libraries, their tops and pages, `FOLDER_RULE`, `folderBlock`), `lib/company/folders.ts` (`fetchFolders`), `app/company/folder-actions.ts` (`createLibraryFolder` · `renameLibraryFolder` · `moveToLibraryFolder` → how many moved / stayed · `deleteLibraryFolder` → what moved up · `undoDeleteLibraryFolder`). **Rules:** Owners and Admins make, rename, move and delete folders; an entry is filed by whoever may rename it (a template's author, an Owner or an Admin; a quote template, which keeps no author, by an Owner or an Admin; an item by the book's rule) — two new rows in Settings › Studio › Who may do what. A folder's name is required, 60 characters at most and not a sibling's (case aside), the message under the field after the first Save. **The kit** (`components/om/folders/`), built from the Files tab's own pieces: `FolderCard` + `FolderGroup` (931:39089 · 931:39084 — moved out of the Files grid; `min` lines them up with a host's cards), `FolderCrumbs` (931:39719 grown: every level on the way a link, **Back up one level**, the open folder's menu through the portal), `MoveDialog` (931:39219 — moved from the Files tab and made generic: folders + entries for orientation, the top named by the host, the name check on its New Folder, none for a viewer who may not make folders), `FolderEmpty`, `FolderPickRow` (the Move dialog's folder row for pickers), `useFolderBrowse` (a picker's browse: folders first — only those with something to pick — the studio default pinned at the top, a search across every folder with `where`). **The galleries:** `LibraryGallery` takes `folders` — New Folder (the Files tab's `create_new_folder` beside New ▾), folder cards first (grid) and folder rows first (list), the crumb above the toolbar inside one (`?folder=`, so Back and a shared link land there), an empty folder that says so, **Move to Folder** on every entry's menu and in a bulk bar (a selection wherever the library has bulk actions or the viewer runs the folders — REG-505's "no bulk action" is over for Owners and Admins; a Designer's list keeps no ticks, as REG-654 decided, and files what they may one at a time), a folder's menu Open · Move · Rename · Delete (Delete says what moves up; Undo); **browse vs find** — with no search, segment or filter the page is the open folder; any of them finds across every folder, each result with its folder on its second line (as typed, not a caps chip). **Templates › Quotes moved onto the shared gallery** (it was the model the others copied, REG-538, and had drifted): same segments, ribbons (top-right where the card takes a tick), status line, "Used by n quotes" link, menus and rules (`deleteBlock` · `foot` · the Deleted sheet's *Deleted by* · *On quotes* heads via `deletedByLabel` · `deletedUsageLabel`), and now sorting, portal menus and the house search-empty state; `templates-table.tsx` · `template-menu.tsx` · `deleted-templates-sheet.tsx` retired. My Sections wear `format_list_bulleted`, no longer the folder glyph. **The pickers:** Choose a template (`TemplatePicker`: `PickerData.folders`, `PickerTemplate.folderId`, through `pickerExtras` — Create Quote, the quote editor, Preview), the contract picker (`fetchContractTemplateOptionsAction` → `{ templates, folders }`), the Tasks tab's Choose Template (`LeadTasksData.templateFolders`), My Templates (T&Cs · Notes; *Create New* inside a folder files it there), the payment presets ▾ (a menu: each folder's splits under its name), the Price book modal (both tabs; REG-662) — `QuoteEditorData.libraryFolders`. The Add Item type-ahead is unchanged. **The Files tab, every gap fixed:** New Folder in the toolbar, the whole way in the crumb, Back up one level, folders first in the list, an empty folder that says so (with Upload Files), a search hit says where it lives (a plate on the tile, a line under the row's name), the folder menu's phone sheet named for the folder (it said "New Folder"), *Delete n items forever?* when folders are among them, a refused move says it failed (the server returned quietly and the tab announced "Moved to …"), folder names checked on the server and in the dialog. **Bug fix found on the way:** the quote editor's *My Templates* did nothing on a collapsed Terms or Notes section — the modal lived inside the collapsed body. | REG-670 | Shell — the rail's panels open at their width | **Company, Tools and View as keep their width after going back** (owner 2026-09-25, with a screenshot of View as at 200 wide and "reports to …" cut: "this issue has been always there, for every refresh this looks correct but when i go back from a screen and click it looks like this"). Cause: each panel's surface wears `leads/menus.module.css` `.menu` (200 wide, a scrolling max-height) and its own `.popover` (304 / 318, no cap) — one class each, so the stylesheet the browser loads LAST wins, and a client-side navigation can land the menus sheet after the panel's. Fix: `.popover.popover` in `company-popover.module.css` and `tools-popover.module.css` (View as wears the Company panel's), the idiom the Items table's `.popoverMenu.popoverMenu` already uses. Probe `design-source/verify/view-as/verify-panel-order.cjs`: normal order 304 · 304 · 318; with the menus sheet forced last 304 · 304 · 318; a single-class 304 rule under that order renders 200 (the owner's screenshot). | bug fix |

| REG-707 | Company › Profile drops the template preview | Owner 2026-09-28: "why do we need "Default template · Default Quote PDF" in this page, if its irrelevant please remove it from this page and adjust the layouts accordingly." The default template's first page at Profile's right came with the brand kit, which moved to Templates › Brand kit (REG-554) and keeps its own preview there; Profile's company details and issuing entities now take the content's full width (REG-661), and the page's doc and loader say so. | user-directed |
| REG-708 | Every page's ghost is its own page, and the loader and the reveal draw the same one | Owner 2026-09-28: "a lot of new pages added to company or other pages the ghost loading is different from the page content and the loader. Please fix this across the pages." Filmed on the production build, a full load of a lead's Files tab painted the **Leads listing's** ghost, then a table ghost, then the file grid; Company pages painted Templates' tab strip over four blank cards, then two tall bones, then the page. **Two causes, two fixes.** (1) A parent's `loading.tsx` wraps its children and paints first on a full load, so each loader now wraps its own page only — the parent pages moved into route groups, the URLs unchanged: `app/leads/(list)` · `app/leads/[id]/(overview)` · `app/quote-x-ray/(home)` · `app/company/plan/(usage)` · `app/company/price-book/(items)` · `app/company/templates/(libraries)` (the five galleries share one ghost; Brand kit has its own) · `app/(editor)/quotes/[quoteId]/(edit)` · `app/(editor)/contracts/[id]/(edit)` · `app/sign/[id]/(sign)`; `app/company/loading.tsx` is gone (Company's index is a redirect). (2) **One ghost per page, drawn by both its `loading.tsx` and its `PageReveal`** — never a count or a variant only the page knows, so the hand-over between the two is invisible — built from the **ghost kit** (`components/shell/ghost-kit.tsx`: the L1 / L2 headers, the tab strip, `Line`, `Slot` — a control's box whose size follows the control's own rules per width and pointer: the 48 field, 36 → 44 selects, the search folding into its glyph at ≤1024, New ▾ folding on a phone — `GhostField`, `GhostTable` with its Add row and its phone cards, and **`Words`**: the page's own sentence in its own class with the letters hidden and each line drawn as a bar, so a help text wraps in the ghost exactly as on the page) on the page's own layout classes. New ghosts: Company › Profile · Members · Questionnaire · Integrations · Plan & AI credits › Usage · Plan & billing · Brand kit · the template galleries · Price book › My Items · My Sections (`components/company/company-ghosts.tsx`, the frame `company-ghost-frame.tsx` draws the L2 header when opened with `?from=`); Materials and the AI Visualizer's home draw the usual page in both (their reveal counted the studio's cards); Leads › Insights (`insights-ghost.tsx`); the rail's Tasks, Tools › Quotes, Tools › Contracts (`components/shell/list-ghosts.tsx`, on the lead tabs' toolbars and tables with the studio columns); **each lead tab's own body** (`components/leads/detail/tab-ghosts.tsx` — the Tasks, Quotes and Contracts tables at their tracks and row heights, Files' folders and tiles, Notes' list and pane with the phone's composer, the question cards beside the summary, the Activity feed; `[tab]/loading.tsx` reads the tab from the address, `DetailTabSkeleton`), the lead strip's eight tab bones (it drew nine since Meetings hid, REG-552), the phone's lead header (the kebab on the title line) and its identity block; Settings per module (`app/settings/settings-ghost.tsx`, `?section=`); the listing by its view (`components/leads/listing-ghost.tsx`: `?view=` or the remembered view — a kanban load streamed the table's ghost, REG-197's gap) at fixed row and card counts (it streamed 8 rows and grew to 12 on hydration); X-Ray's setup scoped and with the intro as the member left it (`xray-setup-ghost.tsx`); the Tasks · T&Cs & Notes · Payments template editors (`library-editor-ghost.tsx`, they drew the quote editor's); the contract editors' Outline pane and meta row; the editors' shared header (`components/shell/editor-head-ghost.tsx`); Preview's bar on its own classes and its A4 at the document's 794; new loaders for the quote a signing link shows and the confirmation link (a blank page before). `PageReveal`'s ghost keeps its own height, as in `loading.tsx`'s flow (stretched to the page's, a column page squeezed its rows and the reveal stopped matching the loader). Verified by capture — the loader (scripts removed), the held reveal and the page, with the ghost laid over the page — at 1440 and 390 touch for 51 routes, and by filming full loads and in-app hops. | user-directed · bug fix |
| REG-709 | The studio-wide search | Owner 2026-09-28: "this search is a global prototype search. I want you to do an amazing global search build … a popover window like what we have for company tab with a search bar and the results shows within the popover window." No mock: built on the house pieces. **Where:** the rail's Search tile (its hint now ⌘K — the page's own search keeps "/"), ⌘K / Ctrl K from any page in the shell, and the phone bar's search glyph; beside the rail in the Company panel's chrome at 560 (a bottom sheet on a phone). **What:** the field (clear, Esc), the scope chips (All · Leads · Quotes · Contracts · Tasks · Notes & Logs · Files · Designs · Templates & Price book · People), the results in groups — Leads, Quotes, Contracts, Tasks, Notes & Logs, Files, Quote X-Ray, AI Visualizer, Templates, Price book, Materials, People and Go to (the app's pages) — the best group first, the typed words marked, a chip for a stage or a status, "Show all n in …" under All; before anything is typed: Recent searches (✕ removes one), Recently opened and Go to (per browser, `om.search.recent` · `om.search.opened`); nothing found says so with a hint and "Search everything". ↑ ↓ move, ↵ opens, ⌘↵ in a new tab, Esc clears then closes; each opening starts fresh. A record opens with `?from=` the page searched from (REG-465); a task, a note, a Price book item or section and a material open on themselves (`?open=<id>`, new on Notes, the Price book and Materials). **The rule:** the house search rule (REG-274) with every word needing a match; Who sees leads applies to leads and everything hanging off them, a proposed Price book item shows only to its maker, an Owner or an Admin, nothing deleted. Code: `lib/global-search/model.ts` (pure) · `queries.ts` (server) · `app/search/actions.ts` · `components/shell/global-search.tsx` (hosted by `AppShell`); probe `design-source/verify/global-search/verify-search.cjs` (27); UAT chapter 15. Invented copy: the placeholder, the scope and group names, "Recent searches", "Recently opened", "Go to", "Show all n in …", the empty and nothing-found lines, the key hints. | user-directed · invented copy |
| REG-710 | The AI credit's level is a ring round the spark | Owner 2026-09-28, after using REG-706: "I'm okay with the ai icon and the container. But the way icon color representation is bad it doesn't seem to look like a AI credit indication by changing the color of it"; of eight options drawn on https://claude.ai/artifact/PFUqY251QUCzAGqFERLkXP she picked **B · Ring round the spark**. `components/om/credit-token.tsx`: the spark is always the mark in full colour (the pale spark behind, the fill from the bottom and the amber / grey recolouring are gone) and the level is a thin ring round it, from 12 o'clock clockwise (`ringOf(size)`; its size since REG-718): the spark's own gradient while there is plenty · an amber arc when low · a red track when the studio is out · an amber track when a member's monthly amount is used. Below 24px (the chip, Plans' lines) it is left off and the number carries the level; `current` (the Generate price, the cost chip) stays a ringless solid spark. The paid row's 8-second turn moves onto the spark alone (`sparkClassName`) — the ring holds still; the upgrade moment and Plans' Done grow the ring from empty (`fillFromEmpty`). The track is the spark's magenta at 16% — a literal, as the Upgrade gradients are (779:19690). UAT 14.01 · 14.07 reworded. | user-directed · invented UI (the option page's drawing, no mock) |
| REG-711 | Keyboard shortcuts: one list, one layer, one guard | Owner 2026-09-28: "the shortcut keys looks good to me. Go ahead and build it all" — the proposal https://claude.ai/artifact/7XFQkqgqwTQujwNRNuqAkh, every question as recommended (Q-26 actions in search: later; Q-27 remapping: no). `lib/keys/registry.ts` declares every key once (id · keys · words · where; `GO`, `MARKED`, `FORMAT_KEYS`, the editors' own lists), `lib/keys/spec.ts` turns a spec into a Mac or Windows keyboard's words, `aria-keyshortcuts` and the WCAG 2.1.4 test, `lib/keys/store.ts` keeps the per-browser state (`om.keys.*`: the two switches, the keys used, the mouse counts, the tip day) and the handlers pages register (`useShortcut`, `useShortcutsHere`). `components/shell/shortcuts/shortcuts-layer.tsx` — mounted by `AppShell` and the editors' layout, never on the client's pages — is the one keyboard: letters listen first (a focused DS button can swallow a key), keys a component may answer itself (⌘Z, arrows, ↵, ⌫, Esc) listen last and stand down; never while typing or composing (IME); an open modal or menu owns the keys; a widget marked `data-kb-own` (the Items table) keeps its own. A key presses the page's own control (`data-kb="<id>"`), so it does what the press does, with the same checks and refusals; lists and tab rows are marked (`data-kb-list` · `data-kb-row` · `data-kb-open` · `data-kb-select`, `data-kb-tabs`). The ? panel (`shortcuts-panel.tsx`, the house non-modal Drawer at 360) lists only what the page in front of you answers, then Everywhere and Dialogs and menus; All pages lists every key; from 1280 the shell's pages make room for it (an editor keeps its own layout — its header is dense — and the panel lies over its right side; a control it covers wears no badge). | user-directed · invented UI (the proposal page, no mock) |
| REG-712 | Keys that work everywhere | Q-02 · Q-11 · Q-16 · Q-17 · Q-23: ? and ⌘/ (⌘/ also while typing) open the ? panel; ⌘K the search — now in the full-page editors too, with the bell (the editors' layout mounts `GlobalSearch`, `NotificationsPanel` and the layer); G then L Leads · T Tasks · Q Quotes · C Contracts · X Quote X-Ray · V AI Visualizer · P Price book · N notifications · S Settings, their places shown after a beat on the snackbar's surface ("G then …"); ⌘Z presses the Undo of the newest snackbar that offers one (`useRunUndo`), after an editor's own ⌘Z has had its turn; Esc closes what is open, then clears the list's cursor and selection, then goes Back (`data-kb="back"` on the L2 header's arrow); ⌘S in an editor says "Saved — everything saves as you type" instead of the browser's Save page (and asks the editor to save now, `om:save-now`); ⌘↵ presses a dialog's primary button (`data-dialog-foot` in the house Dialog). | user-directed · invented copy |
| REG-713 | Keys on lists and on a lead | Q-12…Q-15 · Q-19: J / K and ↓ / ↑ walk a list with a cursor (an outline drawn over the page, so rows, cards and a board show it the same way), ↵ opens the row, X ticks it, ⌘A ticks every row shown, ⌫ presses the selection's Delete — which asks first, as the press does (Delete Forever never gets a key) — Esc clears; C presses what the page creates with (Create New Lead through the listing's own handler, REG-610's C folded in; Add Task, Create Quote, New Contract, New File, New, a library's New), F Filters, V the other view. Marked: the Leads table and board, a lead's Tasks · Quotes · Contracts · Files (grid: ↵ opens as a double-click does) · Notes, /tasks, Tools › Quotes · Contracts, every Company library (cards, rows, folders), the two bulk bars. On a lead: 1–8 switch its tabs (the numbers switch every tab row: Company, Settings, the quote editor's sections), M the stage menu, A Assign (under the stage button while the info column is folded), E Edit, J / K the next and previous lead of the list you came from, on the same tab (`om.kb.trail`, per tab; "That was the last lead in the list"). | user-directed · invented copy |
| REG-714 | Keys in the editors | Q-18 · Q-23 · Q-24: P opens Preview in the quote, contract and template editors; the quote editor's section tabs take 1–8; ⌘F opens the Items table's Find instead of the browser's, while the Items card is open; one Undo in every editor — ⌘Z, ⇧⌘Z and ⌘Y (⌘Y added to the AI Visualizer, the contract paper and Terms / Notes), and every Undo tooltip names its keys (the Items toolbar and its kebab, the text tools, the template editor, the visualizer — they read "Undo (⌘Z)", Mac-only); the ? panel lists each editor's own keys while it is on screen (`GRID_KEYS`, `DOC_KEYS`, `TEMPLATE_KEYS`, `VISUALIZER_KEYS`). | user-directed |
| REG-715 | Learning the keys | Q-03 · Q-05…Q-10: every control with a key shows it in its tooltip (`Tooltip keys` / `shortcut`, with `aria-keyshortcuts` on the control) and at the right of its menu row (`DropdownItem keys` / `shortcut`); an empty, unfocused search box shows "/" at its end (`components/om/slash-slot.tsx`); while the ? panel is out the page's own controls wear their keys (badges drawn over the page; a covered control wears none); the third time the mouse does what a key does earns one tip — "Next time, press F for Filters." with All shortcuts — once per key, at most one a day, never over a dialog; the panel ticks the keys you have used and counts them ("You use 3 of 21 here"); a key pressed with the panel out flashes its row. Doors to the panel: the search's Go to › Keyboard shortcuts ("Keys for this page and every other") and its footer ("? all shortcuts"), Maison AI's "Keyboard shortcuts for this page" (off touch screens), every tip, and a one-time bell note "Keyboard shortcuts are here · Press ? on any page to see them" (repair R64: one stored row per active member, kind `shortcuts_ready`, not a Settings event; the baseline carries it). | user-directed · invented copy |
| REG-716 | Keys that never surprise | Q-20…Q-22 · WCAG 2.1.4: the single-key switch at the foot of the ? panel (per browser, on by default) stops letters, numbers, ? and "/" — arrows, ↵, Esc and ⌘ keys keep working; a Tips switch beside it. Touch screens draw no key chips (`Keys`), until a real keyboard has pressed a key outside a field (`html[data-hw-keyboard]`, a tablet with a keyboard); the client's pages (signing, confirmation, the render link) have no keyboard layer at all. | user-directed |
| REG-717 | Ctrl on Windows, ⌘ on a Mac | Q-25: every key hint is drawn by `components/om/keys.tsx` (the search footer's chip), which reads the computer once — ⌘ ⌥ ⇧ ↵ ⌫ on a Mac, Ctrl Alt Shift Enter Backspace elsewhere — and the server draws the Mac's until the browser redraws. The rail's chip reads Ctrl+K on Windows; the search footer's ⌘↵ and the tooltips' Undo (⌘Z) follow. | bug fix · user-directed |
| REG-718 | The credit ring at the rail icons' size | Owner 2026-09-28, on REG-710's rail: "can you make the circle and the icon little smaller because it looks bigger compared to other icons in the sidebar". The ring now lies inside its box, 1px in, as a glyph's ink does (`ringOf`: a 22 ring, stroke 2, in the rail's 24 box — it was a 30 ring spilling round the box), and the spark inside it is half the box (`SPARK_IN_RING`, 12px in the rail; it was 18). Alone — the chip, Plans' lines — the spark keeps the Free gem's size (`SPARK`, REG-706). The same proportions at every size: the More row's 36, the credits card's 40 (stroke 3), Plans' Done 88. Shots `design-source/verify/keyboard/ring-*-2x.png`. | user-directed |
| REG-719 | A room's ⋯ inside its own chip | Owner 2026-09-28, on the AI Visualizer's rooms row: "this contextual menu popover is confusing since it's placed outside the each room container i think its better to move inside the container for each room". Each room chip carries its own ⋯ (`RoomMore` in `components/visualizer/editor/topbar.tsx`), in the desktop bar and the narrow rooms row alike; the detached ⋯ after the chips is gone. The menu (Rename · Change photo · Delete room) opens under the ⋯ pressed, and pressing another room's ⋯ picks that room first — the menu acts on the room it sits in. The house `Chips` takes `trail` for this: the chip becomes a box holding its radio and the action (`data-chip-box`; the picked fill, stroke and hover on the box; the arrows still walk the radios); the picked room's ⋯ is the Tab stop. UAT 13.03 reworded. | user-directed |

A dialog footer made of buttons only now wraps rather than clip (`.foot:not(:has(> :not(button)))`): on a 320 phone "Keep Pro — Studio" had been cut off by the card's edge; a footer that fits is unchanged. The L2 crumb reads "{where Back goes} / Company"; when Back goes to another Company page, the section's word repeated the frame's. That page is now named by its section — "Plan & AI credits / Company / Profile" (`companySectionOf` in `lib/company/sections.ts`, `originOf` in `components/company/company-shell.tsx`); the same for Settings' own modules ("Quotes & pricing / Settings"). Every other origin reads as before ("Quotes / Company", "Settings / Company"). | bug fix |

They are now two stacks, as the lead Overview's card grid (858:24888): the plan and its payment card on the left, the top-ups and the billing details on the right, 16 between cards in each (`.cards` · `.cardCol` in `components/company/plan/plan.module.css`). The table copied the Qanvast Ops card's stylesheet but took only the first of its two `.hCell` rules, so each label sat in the top-left corner of its track, in black, 16px left of its column's values. The head cell is now the listing's — 16/24/16/16, the label centred in the 56, the head's grey, 12/500 uppercase with an ellipsis (`components/leads/insights/insights-view.module.css`) — and the frozen Member column draws the listing's edge again (the 1px rule at rest, the shadow only while scrolled sideways), also lost in the copy. Three floors grew so every label reads in full at the narrowest (Assign → First outreach 220 · First outreach → Won 200 · Awaiting outreach 180; the ghost's columns follow). Every other table's head was checked: only this one had the gap. Probe `design-source/verify/insights-head-2026-09-28/verify-insights-head.cjs` (16). | bug fix |
| REG-724 | The L2 ghost's head is the page's | Found while checking REG-723's ghost: the ghost kit's L2 head (`GhostHead2`, Insights and every Company page opened with `?from=`) put a 4px gap between the crumb and the title, so it stood 76 against the page's 72 and every block under it rose 4px at the hand-off; on a phone it kept the header's actions beside the title, 36px short of the page, where the page gives them the whole second line (Profile opened from Plan & billing: Discard · Save changes). Both now as the page (`components/shell/ghost-kit.module.css` `.head2Title`, `.head2 .headActions` ≤767). Ghost and page heads measured equal on five routes at 390 and 1440; loader and reveal still identical on all 17 Company and Insights routes (REG-708). | bug fix |
| REG-725 | Every overlay wears one head and one foot | Owner 2026-09-29: "there are modals with bottom border and there are without. Not just the modal window, there are a lot of inconsistency across the components. Can you please fix all of this once for all" — and, asked, the divider rule: **Always** (of always · only while scrolling · never). The audit (`design-source/context/consistency/01-consistency-audit-2026-09-29.md`) found 93 dialogs in five head shapes (compact without the rule and a 16px title, form 72/84 with no rules, standard 57 with a border, the templates modals without the rule, modal 64 with it), 15 drawers with a 65 head and a rounded close, and 17 hand-rolled overlays with heads, closes and feet of their own (four close hovers, no elevation on 14, z 100 against 110). Now ONE anatomy, drawn in one place: `SurfaceHead` · `SurfaceFoot` · `FootStart` · `surfaceClasses` in `components/om/dialog.tsx` — head 64 (p20/24), the 20/24 bold title, the bare 24 outlined close, the 1px rule inside the head always; body p24; foot 68 (p16/24, gap 12) right-aligned, its top rule always, its shadow only while the body scrolls (REG-322). Every `Dialog` size, `ConfirmDialog`, the `Drawer` and the hand-rolled overlays (the lead form, the four Filters, Import, Review, Create Quote, Attach files, the task templates, the three Deleted sheets, Return details and Return Lead, Move to folder, Move stage, the tasks and assign popovers, the file viewer's panels) render them; the sizes now differ only in width and in how a phone shows them. Confirms: the six Lead Details deletes are `ConfirmDialog`s — "No · Yes" became the verb ("Cancel · Delete", "Cancel · Move to Trash", REG-720's rule) — and every confirm's message is 14/20 in the text's ink (902:27738's, over 779:24663's 12/16 grey); every centred alert keeps 24px phone margins; confirms over a sheet are `nested`. | user-directed · invented copy |
| REG-726 | The design checks: the house rules as code | Owner 2026-09-29: "i don't this to happen in the future. How can we avoid all the inconsistency for the future developments? … when the team take this repo and integrate it breaks." Every drift the audit found was a copy, and nothing failed when a rule was broken. `npm run design:lint` (`scripts/design-lint.mjs`) reads the code against eight rules — a modal draws the house head and foot · a close is the house one · colours, type and radii from the tokens · no design values in inline styles · the DS through `@/components/ds` · no copied table stylesheets — per file, against `design-lint.baseline.json`, which holds today's violations and may only go down (a new one fails with the file, the line and the house piece to use). `npm run design:anatomy` (`scripts/design-anatomy.mjs`) measures the running app: every page's table heads against the leads listing's and every dialog the pages open (C / F, REG-711) against the house head and foot, at 1440 and 390. CI runs both on every push and pull request; the PR template, `CONTRIBUTING.md` §5–7 (a new *Integrating 1.0 without breaking the design*) and `AGENTS.md` name them. CODEOWNERS already names both of the owner's accounts; branch protection on `main` (a pull request, the CI check, review from Code Owners) is hers to switch on. | user-directed |
| REG-727 | Tables: one core, two types | Owner 2026-09-29, "as recommended" (design-source/context/consistency/01-…). Every data table composes components/om/table-core.module.css (the listing's head, cells, row states incl. selected + hover, frozen edge, row actions 24 in) and keeps only its mocked deltas; the thirteen copies are gone (lint `copied-table` 13 → 0). Lists inside Settings / Company cards are the one card table (components/om/card-table.module.css, `data-card-table`). Both cores sit in CSS cascade layers, so a table's own rule always wins whatever the bundle order (a build put the Quotes CSS before the core and its phone rule lost). Insights and Plan are cards on a phone; the Deleted / Archived Quotes & Contracts sheets take the Deleted trio's shape and go full screen on a phone (`Dialog phoneFull`); the phone ring round card stacks is gone; Notifications' switches no longer clip on a phone; X-Ray's Status track 184; the Contracts head 56 (its mock 931:38690 draws 48 — kept at the house 56 for one anatomy, rows keep the mock's 68). | user-directed · bug fix |
| REG-728 | Menus: one row, one scale, one layer | DropdownItem `help` · `meta` · `selected`, a disabled row's reason printed in the row (never a native title); DropdownLabel / DropdownDivider (4px); MENU_WIDTH 200 · 240 · 320; row menus through PortalMenu everywhere; the stage menu on house rows with arrow keys; menus and popovers above the Maison AI button, the snackbar above nested dialogs; every row on a phone sheet on the 16 gutter; the date picker a bottom sheet on a phone; one rail-popover hook. | user-directed |
| REG-729 | Buttons, chips, links, page structure | ScopePill for every scope pill (Tasks gains its applied dot); Chips in every filter; MetaChip / DS Tag r4 for every tag; one SelectTrigger (36 / 28) for the compact pickers; one text link (components/om/text-link.module.css); the Lead Details bulk bar for both bars; kebabs 36 in rows, 24 in heads; round icon buttons r4; the Visualizer's text buttons 600; one tab toolbar, one Filters shell, one empty card; Import History on PageHeaderL2; Company / Settings card titles heading-h4 with body-s help; no letter-spacing on uppercase captions. | user-directed · invented copy (labels on text areas) |
